🤖 RAG ANSWER (75.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program at Sabancı University, follow
   these steps:  1. **Check eligibility**: Undergraduate students must have a
   minimum GPA of 2.20, and graduate students a GPA of 2.5, with an
   internship acceptance letter from a host organization for at least 2
   months.  2. **Submit documents**: By October, submit your CV, transcript,
   and the signed acceptance letter to the Internship Office. Undergraduate
   applicants must also provide ENG 101-102 course grades or SPS/AL course
   grades for language evaluation.  3. **Complete agreements**: Secure the
   signed Erasmus+ Internship Mobility Agreement from the host organization,
   and finalize the Learning Agreement, Grant Agreement, and Travel/Insurance
   documents.  4. **Online application**: Submit all required documents via
   the Online Application System and notify the Internship Office.  5.
   **Selection process**: Applicants are ranked by Erasmus+ Score, and the
   Student Selection Committee approves the final list for grant allocation.
   Results are announced by mid-April.   Applications open in late October,
   with deadlines and procedures detailed on the internal web and internship
   websites.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (111.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe, yurtdışında geçirilecek süre ve
   Ulusal Ajans'ın belirlediği aylık hibe miktarına göre hesaplanır.
   Hareketlilik süresi tam aylarla ifade edilir; tamamlanmayan aylar için
   aylık birim masrafın 1/30’u, harcanan gün sayısıyla çarpılır. Öğrenci,
   Erasmus+ AB fonlarından mali destek alıyorsa, hibe süresi hareketliliğin
   toplam süresine eşit olur. Eğer sıfır-hibe dönemleri varsa, asgari
   hareketlilik süresi (öğrenim için 3 ay veya 1 akademik dönem) dikkate
   alınır. Hibeler, ev sahibi ülkeye göre değişen aylık tutarlarla belirlenir
   ve ödeme, hareketlilik başlamadan önce yapılır. Hibe miktarı, Ulusal Ajans
   tarafından her yıl duyurulan standartlara göre hesaplanır. Katılımcı, Hibe
   Sözleşmesi ve Öğrenim Anlaşması imzalama zorunluluğuna tabidir. Toplam
   hibe, hareketlilik döneminin ay ve gün sayısına göre Ulusal Ajans
   kuralları çerçevesinde belirlenir.
------------------------------------------------------------

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (50.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students at Sabancı
   University involves several steps. Students prepare the LA, specifying
   courses to be taken abroad, with approval from their Exchange Academic
   Advisor, who verifies course equivalencies (credits, types, and ECTS
   values). The student submits the signed LA to the partner institution for
   approval and then to the International Relations Office (IRO). For
   graduate students, the LA must detail the research/project during mobility
   and be submitted online within the IRO’s timeline. After submission, the
   IRO forwards the LA to the Faculty Administrative Officer for final
   approval by the Faculty Executive Committee or Faculty Administrative
   Board. Students must ensure selected courses align with SU graduation
   requirements and inform their advisor of any schedule changes before
   completing the term abroad. Upon return, students must submit the signed
   LA, partner university transcript, and Certificate of Attendance to IRO.
------------------------------------------------------------

   cezasına neden olur. Bu ceza, sınavlarda kopya çekmek veya çektirmek fiili
   için belirlenmiştir. Ayrıca, sınavlarda kopyaya teşebbüs etmek, **1
   haftadan 1 aya kadar** süreyle uzaklaştırma cezasına tabidir. Bu süreler,
   disiplin yönergesinde açıkça belirtilmiştir.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (80.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary objection process at Sabancı University involves
   submitting a written objection to the University Administrative Board
   within **15 days** of the punishment notification. The board reviews the
   objection and either upholds or rejects the sanction within **15 days**.
   If rejected, the disciplinary board or supervisor finalizes the decision
   based on the rejection grounds. For housing-related issues, objections to
   warnings or reprimands must be submitted to the Disciplinary Board within
   **5 working days**, with a decision made within **7 working days**.
   Appeals to administrative jurisdiction can bypass the objection process,
   and if a stay of execution is granted, the Student Registry (SR) notifies
   relevant parties **within 1 day** of receiving the court order. Suspension
   periods for disciplinary punishment are not counted in the maximum study
   period, and suspended students must pay tuition for the affected semester.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Disiplin suçlarında zamanaşımı süreleri, ceza türüne göre değişir. Uyarma,
   kınama veya yükseköğretim kurumundan bir haftadan bir aya kadar
   uzaklaştırma cezaları için, fiilin işlendiği tarihten itibaren en geç 1 ay
   içinde disiplin soruşturması başlatılmazsa yetki zamanaşımına uğrar. Bir
   veya iki yarıyıl uzaklaştırma ile çıkarma cezaları için bu süre 3 aydır.
   Ancak, cinsel taciz/saldırı gibi fiillerde zamanaşımı süresi 2 yıldır ve
   bu süre, eylemin işlendiği tarihten itibaren soruşturmanın tamamlanması ve
   karara bağlanması için geçerlidir. Adli yargı hükmüne ihtiyaç duyulan
   durumlarda, zamanaşımı süresi kesinleşen hükmün tarihiyle başlar. Disiplin
   Kurulu kararları, genellikle 10 gün içinde verilir; bu süre, soruşturma
   tamamlandıktan sonra başlar.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (67.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Sabancı University Main Endowment Scholarship Fund is a financial aid
   initiative designed to support undergraduate and graduate students who
   demonstrate academic success and financial need. It is expanded through
   donations from Sabancı University employees, alumni, students, and
   international contributors via the "Friends of Sabancı University Fund"
   under the Turkish Philanthropic Fund in New York. The fund accepts cash
   and in-kind donations, with administrative processes managed by the
   Institutional Partnerships and Alumni Relations (IPAR) unit and the Human
   Resources (HR) department. Donations from employees are deducted from
   their salaries at a self-determined rate, while international
   contributions leverage U.S. tax incentives. The Board of Trustees oversees
   the fund's governance, and revenue from university venue rentals may also
   contribute to its resources.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Disiplin cezası alan öğrencinin bursu, ceza türüne göre farklı şekilde
   etkilenir:  1. **Kınama veya 1-1.5 ay uzaklaştırma cezası** alırsa, burs
   kesintisiz devam eder.  2. **1 veya 2 dönem uzaklaştırma cezası**
   verilirse, ceza süresince burs kesilir ve öğrenci, öğrenim ücretinin
   %25’si öder. Ceza sona erdikten sonra burs yeniden bağlanır.  3.

   Mezuniyet durumunda, ceza süresi için ödenmesi gereken ek ücreti,
   mezuniyetten sonra 1 ay içinde Burs Ana Varlık Fonu’na ödemek zorundadır.
   4. Disiplin cezası alan öğrenciler, burs başvurularında **avantajsız
   değerlendirilir**.  Bu uygulamalar, lisans ve lisansüstü öğrenciler için
   geçerlidir. Her dönem sonunda disiplin cezası alan öğrencilerin burs
   durumu Burs ve Mali Destek Yönergesi’ne göre yeniden değerlendirilir.

[Graduate Program Opening]

[Pr...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (58.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, the Graduate School Board provides the
   required information in the YOKSIS system, including courses to be taken,
   total credits for graduation, and the program's purpose. The proposal is
   submitted for approval with the opinion of the UEC under the main
   coordination of the VP-E and the approval of the Academic Council and the
   Board of Trustees. Education begins once the program is approved by YOK.
   For closure, the Graduate School Board, with the UEC's opinion, submits
   justifications for the Academic Council's approval, which then forwards
   the proposal to the Board of Trustees for final approval. The SR prepares
   a letter explaining the closure and submits it to the President for
   signature. After YOK's approval, the SR ensures the closure is reflected
   in the YOKSIS system within one week.
------------------------------------------------------------

📊 VERIFIED FACTS (From Knowledge Graph):

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Value: two decimals
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account
  • Requirement: tekrar edilen derslerden alınan en son not dikkate alınır (lisans)

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Graduate Education and Training Instruction Letter → [req...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (58.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University calculates GPA by multiplying the SU credit of each
   course by its grade coefficient, summing these total points, and dividing
   by the total SU credits of all courses taken in the relevant term. For
   undergraduate programs, the Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses
   taken since enrollment, using the same method but incorporating all
   courses in the calculation. Repeated courses are counted with the last
   grade received. Graduate programs also calculate GPA using SU credits and
   coefficients, with CGPA similarly including all courses, but academic
   rankings consider all digits after the decimal point rather than rounding
   to two decimals. Both undergraduate and graduate GPA calculations are
   based on SU credits assigned under the National Credit System.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Leave Of Absence Procedure]
  • Deadline: 1 ay
  • Deadline: 1 dönem
  • Step: İlgili Birim Yöneticisi'ne yazılı olarak bildirilir
  • Step: İstek Rektör/GS ve İK Direktörü ile görüşülür ve bir karara varılır

  • faculty member → [responsible for] → process t...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (60.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Leave of Absence process for students at Sabancı University requires
   submitting a request by the last business day of the 4th week following
   the first day of classes in the academic year after registration (25% of
   the semester or academic year tuition is paid if accepted), or by the 7th
   week (50% payment). Undergraduate students must provide documented reasons
   (health, financial, family, academic, or unforeseen circumstances) and,
   for cases like conscription or legal issues, apply within 2 months of the
   situation arising. Graduate students apply to their Graduate School
   Administrative Board for approval, with similar reasons and a 25% payment
   requirement if leaving for a semester. If a graduate student returns
   during leave, the paid leave fee is deducted from the tuition for the
   semester they re-enroll in. Leave requests must specify exact dates, and
   unpaid leave without return within 3 business days may result in contract
   termination for staff, but this does not apply to students.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (38.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include:  1.
   **Warnings, reprimands, and temporary suspensions** (1 week to 1 month)
   imposed by Deans or Graduate School Directors for minor violations.  2.
   **Suspension from the university** for 1 or 2 semesters, with the
   suspension period excluded from the maximum study duration and requiring
   payment of tuition for the affected semester.  3. **Dismissal** from the
   university for severe violations, with no campus access during the penalty
   period.  4. **Dormitory-specific penalties** such as removal from student
   residences, with objections to warnings/reprimands reviewed by the
   Disciplinary Board within 7 working days.  5. **Repeated violations**
   result in a one-grade heavier penalty, though dismissal cannot be applied
   for a repeat offense.  6. Notifications to scholarship providers,
   dormitory management, and psychological services are mandatory for
   suspensions lasting at least one semester.  7. Students under suspension
   or dismissal are prohibited from entering the university campus.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (81.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Bir dersin tekrar edilebileceği dönem sayısı, program türüne göre değişir.
   Lisans öğrencileri için ders tekrarı, daha önce geçer not almış oldukları
   dersi, aldıkları dönemi izleyen en çok üç dönem içerisinde
   tekrarlayabilirler. Lisansüstü öğrencilerin tekrar hakkı için belirtilen
   bir dönem sayısı sınırlaması yoktur; bu öğrenciler, "C, C+” veya “B-” notu
   aldıkları dersleri, programdaki dersleri tamamlama süresinin sonuna kadar
   tekrar alabilir. Ancak, lisans öğrencileri için "F", "U", "NA" veya "W"
   notu alanlar, zorunlu dersleri tekrar etmek zorunda kalabilirken, seçmeli
   derslerin yerine danışman onayıyla başka dersler alabilir. Lisansüstü
   öğrencileri için "F", "U", "NA", "D", "D+" veya "C-" notu alanlar, o
   dersleri tekrar almak zorundadır. Ders tekrarında, daha önce alınan dersin
   puanı GNO hesaplamalarından düşürülür ve en son dersin puanı hesaplama
   sürecine dahil edilir. Belgede bir dersin tekrar edilebileceği **maksimum
   kez sayısı** açıkça belirtilmemiştir.
------------------------------------------------------------

   saatten önce iptal %100 ücret). Araç, genellikle sabah teslim edilir;

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (88.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To initiate the graduation audit process, prospective graduate students
   must submit their applications to the Student Resources (SR) through the
   SIS system within the academic calendar-specified dates. The SR
   collaborates with the relevant Faculties/Institutes to verify if students
   meet graduation requirements, including completed courses, SU/ECTS
   credits, and grade point averages, which are monitored every semester by
   both the student and the Institute. For thesis-based graduate programs,
   the thesis advisor, student, and Institute oversee stages like proposal,
   defense, and submission. Institutes submit approved graduation lists to
   SR, ensuring students are registered or participating in an exchange
   program to qualify as candidates. Students who fail the audit must reapply
   for the next semester, and diplomas are issued after fulfilling all
   conditions, with signatures from the President, Graduate School Director,
   and SR Director on postgraduate diplomas.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (52.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The lost and found procedure on campus involves the following steps:  1.
   Individuals who find items should report them by calling extension
   **3565** or delivering them to the **Security Office in Building D2**.  2.
   Security officers prepare a **Found Item Return Form (two copies)**,
   signed by the finder and the officer. The original remains with the
   Security Unit, while the second copy is given to the finder.  3. Found
   items are categorized as **valuable (e.g., electronics, money, precious
   metals)** or non-valuable, stored in labeled bags, and numbered by the
   Security Directorate.  4. Items are retained for **1 year**. For lost
   items on campus, users must notify the **Security Unit (tel: 5555)** and
   the **IT Unit** via their system.  5. Subcontractor/tenant companies must
   deliver forgotten items to the Security Administration **on the same day**
   they are found, using extension 3565 for contact.   This process ensures
   proper documentation, storage, and retrieval of lost items.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (104.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Uzaklaştırma cezası alan öğrencilere herhangi bir ücret iadesi yapılmaz.
   Disiplin suçu nedeniyle Üniversiteden uzaklaştırılan öğrenciler, ödemekle
   yükümlü oldukları dönem öğrenim ücretini tamamıyla ödemek zorundadır. Bu
   durum, "tgy_tr_v23" belgesinde açıkça belirtilmiştir. Ayrıca, uzaklaştırma
   süresi azami öğrenim süresine eklenmez ve bu süre içinde öğrenci,
   Üniversite olanaklarından yararlanamaz. Burs durumu için ise, bir dönem
   veya iki dönem uzaklaştırma cezası alan öğrencilerin bursu kesilir; ancak
   bu durumda öğrenim ücretinin %25’si ödenir. Öğrenci, disiplin cezası
   nedeniyle ödenmesi gereken ek ücretleri mezuniyetten sonra da ödemekle
   yükümlü olabilir.
------------------------------------------------------------

   tarafından aylık onaylanır.  3. **Akşam yemeği**: Seçmeli yemek ve
   indirimli set menüler sunulur.  4. **Kumanya**: Vardiyalı çalışanlar için
   her gün değişen kahvaltılık malzemelerden oluşur.  5. **Kira işletmeleri
   hizmeti**: Belirli saat aralıklarında yiyecek, içecek ve paket servisi
   sunulur.  6. **İçme suyu hizmeti**: Kampüs genelinde su sebilleri ile
   sağlanır.   Öğrenciler ve çalışanlar, kartlarında yüklü bakiyeyi
   kullanarak indirimli set menüleri ve yemekleri faydalanabilir. Vardiyalı
   çalışanlar özel kumanya hizmetinden yararlanır.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (114.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University offers several research project fund types, including
   the **President’s Office Research Fund**, which supports full-time faculty
   members who have completed six months at the university and started within
   the last two years (or those who previously received an Integration
   Project), with a one-time application limit. The **Project Research Fund
   (PAF)** is derived from accumulated research personnel budgets or
   remaining project funds, allocated for research purposes. Additionally,
   the **Personal Research Fund (PRF)** is available for individual research
   activities, governed by specific usage principles. Lastly, the **SOP
   Fund** pertains to the Industry-Focused Projects Program, covering
   undergraduate courses (ENS 491 and ENS 492) involving collaborations with
   industry institutions. Each fund has distinct eligibility and application
   criteria.
------------------------------------------------------------

   include university shuttle services for all members to and from the
   campus, private student shuttles to coordinate with companies, and on-

   purposes such as artistic, sportive, academic, or social activities.

   subcontractors. Vehicle allocation and temporary vehicle services are

------------------------------------------------------------

   transfer credits back to Sabancı University. According to the Disciplinary
   Procedure document, students who have received punishments of Suspension

   applies regardless of whether the student is undergraduate or graduate.

   olurken bilgi merkezindeki borç veya iade edilmemiş kitap durumu kontrol
   edilir; bu işlemler tamamlanmadan mezuniyet formu imzalanmaz.
   Kütüphanelerarası ödünç hizmetinden yararlanan öğrenciler, bilgi kaynağını
   iade etmeyen veya hasar veren durumlarda da mezuniyet belgelerinin
   alınması engellenebilir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (45.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged
   through the Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit. Units must submit the
   **Special Event and Catering Request Form** on SUForm **30 days before the
   event**. FBS evaluates and approves suitable catering companies every 3
   years based on hygiene, service, and equipment standards. After form
   submission, FBS provides a preliminary offer, and the Procurement and
   Operations Unit (POP) handles procurement. The selected company’s details
   are reported to the unit, and FBS coordinates event execution, including
   menu specifications and operational measures. Units cannot request advance
   procurement without the formal request form.
------------------------------------------------------------

   uzmanı veya göğüs hastalıkları uzmanı tarafından imzalanmış** olması
   şarttır.  3. **Akciğer filmi en fazla 6 ay önce** çekilmiş olmalıdır.  4.
   **İşe başlama gününde** doldurulmuş sağlık sigortası başvurusu yapılır.
   Daha önce özel sağlık sigortası olan çalışanlar, **30 gün içinde** eski
   sigorta sertifikasının fotokopisini sunmalıdır.  5. Sigorta şirketi,
   geçmiş hastalıklarla ilgili **doktor raporu talep edebilir**. Talep
   geldiğinde çalışan, raporu broker şirketi aracılığıyla sigorta şirketine
   gönderir.  6. Tüm evraklar tamamlandıktan sonra **sigorta girişi yapılır**
   ve çalışan bilgilendirilir.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (96.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Rectorate Research Fund (SU President’s Office Research Fund) requires
   the following:  1. **Eligibility**: Full-time faculty members who have
   started at Sabancı University in the last two years and completed six
   months at the institution. Faculty who previously received an Integration
   Project are also eligible but can apply only once.  2. **Project
   Requirements**:   - Submit at least two new documented external grant
   applications or secure one guaranteed external financing by the project’s
   end.   - Publish high-impact scientific articles.  3. **Budget and
   Duration**:   - Maximum duration of 36 months.   - Theoretical research
   budgets up to 300,000 TL; experimental research (if >30% of costs are for
   lab equipment, consumables, or field studies) up to 600,000 TL.  4.
   **Compatibility**: The proposed project must align with the applicant’s
   ongoing or initial research activities.  5. **Infrastructure Focus**:
   Budget prioritization must emphasize infrastructure (devices, consumables,
   outsourcing) rather than personnel salaries.
------------------------------------------------------------
